    第八节：法定执业范围、督导机制与授权委派原则 (Page 13)

    执业范围与严禁越权 (Scope of Practice and Limitations)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文Support workers must understand what to do, what not to do, and the limit and extent of a health care worker's role as determined by their province's educational standards and employer's policies in order to protect clients from harm. The term scope of practice is not a copyrighted term, and it is not intended only for regulated professionals. Instead, it is used to identify what a support worker may and may not do in relation to client care.

    Never act beyond the legal limits of your role. Also, never perform a function or task that you have not been trained to do. If you perform a task that is outside these limits, you could harm a client and create serious legal problems for yourself and your employer.

    CN 译文为了切实保护服务对象免受人身伤害，支持工作者必须深刻理解自己“该做什么”、“绝不该做什么”，以及由其所在省份教育培训大纲和雇主管理规章制度所界定的职责边界与权限范畴。“法定执业范围”（Scope of Practice）并非受版权保护的专属词汇，它绝非仅适用于受监管的注册医护人员；恰恰相反，在全行业中，它被广泛用于精准界定支持工作者在照护案主时“被法律允许执行的操作”与“被法律严格禁止执行的行为”。

    绝不可越权执行超出你法定角色界限的任何操作！同时，绝对严禁执行你从未接受过正规院校临床技能培训的任何技术性任务！如果你胆敢越过这一红线擅自操作，你极有可能直接导致案主伤残甚至死亡，并为你自己以及你的受雇机构招致灾难性的重大法律诉讼追责与刑事责任。

    执业范围的三大决定性权威来源 (Three Sources of Scope of Practice)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文The support worker's scope of practice in any province, territory, city, or municipality is determined from three important sources. Those sources include the following:

    1. Provincial/territorial educational standard of practice: Many provinces now follow either the national or provincial program standards, which dictate what support workers need to be taught in their educational programs. The support worker educational program includes information on the scope of practice for support work in that province or territory. Students will learn about the laws and the support worker's professional responsibilities in their part of the country. Students should ask their instructors if they require any clarification. (Please visit [Nova Scotia CCA Scope of Practice Document](http://www.novascotiacca.ca/DocumentViewer.aspx?elementId=106908&portalName=ha) to view an example of a scope of practice document for the province of Nova Scotia.) 2. Employer's policies: Every employer has written policies that establish what can and cannot be done by support workers. These policies should be read carefully before starting work. 3. Supervisor: On the job, the supervisor is the best source of clarification of the employer's policies. It is far better to ask for direction than risk harming a client, so never hesitate to ask questions or request clarification about any procedure.

    CN 译文在任何省份、地区、城市或市镇，支持工作者的执业范围均由以下三大核心支柱权威决定： 1. 各省/地区官方教育大纲与执业标准（Educational Standards）：加拿大许多省份目前均严格遵循国家级或省级教学大纲标准，明确规制院校在支持工作者培训项目中必须教授的理论与操作技能。学生将在学习中系统掌握所在地区的法律法规及法定职责界限。若有任何疑问，学生应当主动向教师请教。（读者可参阅加拿大新斯科舍省继续照料助理 CCA 官方执业范围文件范本）。 2. 雇主机构的管理规章制度（Employer's Policies）：每一家正规医疗机构或社区机构都拥有详尽的书面规章制度，黑纸白字地明确界定该机构内支持工作者被允许执行与严禁涉足的任务清单。在正式上岗提供照护前，必须逐条研读这些制度。 3. 临床主管督导（Supervisor）：在临床第一线，督导护士是解答与澄清雇主制度疑难的最权威来源。主动向上级护士请教指导，其安全价值远远高于冒险违规操作而致案主于险境！因此，在对任何临床操作的合规性产生哪怕一丝疑虑时，绝不可盲目臆断，必须毫不犹豫地向上级请示。

    职业素养提升、终身学习与反思性执业回顾 (Professional Development and Lifelong Learning)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文Being a professional requires critical thinking and reflective practice review. Critical thinking is the ability to think clearly and logically, using reflection and reason, based on knowledge obtained from experience, observation, or education. A reflective practice review is a self-evaluation of care provided with the goal of identifying ways to improve clinical performance and caregiving.

    Health care is an ever-changing field. It is the responsibility of every professional in the field to ensure that all clinical skills and knowledge are up to date, since their own health and safety and the health and safety of co-workers and clients are at stake. Depending on the region, many postgraduate certificate courses and workshops may be available to support workers, such as advanced palliative care, conflict management courses, or medication management. Support workers also need to be aware of the scope of practice in their province or territory, since some, in particular situations, have been asked to perform tasks that are not within the support worker's scope.

    CN 译文成为一名真正的专业从业者，离不开临床批判性思维与持续的反思性执业回顾。批判性思维（Critical Thinking）是指立足于从实践经验、敏锐观察或正规教育中获取的专业知识，运用反思与理性逻辑进行清晰、严密推理思考的心智能力。反思性执业回顾（Reflective Practice Review）是指从业人员在完成临床照护后对自己所提供的服务进行自我剖析与自省评估，其终极目标在于发现自身不足并找出持续优化临床执业表现与照护技巧的具体途径。

    医疗保健是一个日新月异、知识技术快速迭代的领域。确保自身的临床技能与前沿照护知识时刻保持在最新状态，是该领域中每一位从业人员神圣不可推卸的职业责任——因为这不仅直接关乎服务对象的安危，亦直接牵涉到同事及支持工作者自身的健康与人身安全。根据所在地区不同，许多针对支持工作者的毕业后进修认证课程和专题工作坊可供深造选修，例如高级安宁舒缓疗护（Advanced Palliative Care）、职场冲突管理（Conflict Management）或药物协助管理（Medication Management）等。支持工作者还必须时刻保持法律敏锐度，时刻清晰知晓本省执业范围红线，因为在某些临床特殊情境下，个别机构或人员可能会违规要求支持工作者去执行并不在其法定执业范围内的危险技术性操作。

    支持工作者的临床督导机制 (The Supervision of Support Workers)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文In facilities, in the community, or in private homes, support workers may be supervised by a nurse or other health care provider. A registered nurse (RN) is licensed and regulated by the province or territory to maintain overall responsibility for the planning and provision of client care. Some RNs have university degrees and even postgraduate education. Others have community college diplomas. RNs assess clients, make nursing diagnoses, develop care plans, and implement and evaluate nursing care. They also carry out physicians' orders. An RN is usually a team leader of the health care team, which includes RPNs or LPNs (see below), support workers, and other allied health care providers.

    A licensed practical nurse (LPN), also known as a registered practical nurse (RPN), is licensed and regulated by the province or territory to carry out basic nursing procedures and provide client care. RPNs and LPNs have a community college diploma, although many also have university degrees and even postgraduate education. Like RNs, they must hold a current nursing registration in the province that they practise in. RPNs or LPNs function in a decision-making position when caring for stable clients with uncomplicated health issues, but when providing care to clients with serious and unstable health issues, they assist RNs and help with complex procedures. RPNs and LPNs often supervise support workers.

    Some clients, particularly in a live-in environment, may be assigned to a primary care nurse, who is responsible for the ongoing management of the health of a client. Duties of this nurse include liaising with other health care team members, the client, and her or his family. In an acute-care environment, the primary care nurse may or may not be directly involved in providing the client care on a daily basis but will oversee the work of those who do, including the support worker.

    In some situations, a support worker may be supervised by a professional other than an RN, RPN, or LPN. For example, in the recreation department of a long-term care facility, support workers may report to a recreational therapist. In the community, a supervisor may be a social worker, a physiotherapist, or another health care provider.

    Some support workers are hired and supervised directly by clients. These support workers must be particularly aware of provincial or territorial legislation that limits the tasks and procedures they can perform.

    CN 译文无论是在院舍机构、社区中心还是私人家庭中，支持工作者通常都在护士或其他专业医护人员的督导下开展工作： 1. 注册护士（RN）督导：注册护士由省或地区法定发牌监管，对服务对象整体照护计划的制定与实施承担全面法律责任。RN 通常拥有四年制护理学士学位甚至研究生学历；他们负责全面评估案主病情、下达护理诊断、制定并动态调整照护计划、组织实施并评价护理成效，并严格执行医师医嘱。RN 通常是跨学科医疗团队的领队，统筹指挥 RPN/LPN、支持工作者及其他相关辅助医护人员。 2. 注册实用护士（RPN / LPN）督导：RPN/LPN 同样由省或地区依法发牌监管，具备公立学院大专文凭。他们与 RN 一样必须持有本省有效的年度注册护士执照。当面对病情稳定、预后明朗的慢性病案主时，RPN/LPN 处于独立的临床决策地位；而在照护病情凶险不稳定的危重患者时，他们协助 RN 并配合完成复杂操作。在长期照护院舍等机构中，RPN/LPN 经常直接担任支持工作者的现场主管护士。 3. 责任制护士（Primary Care Nurse）：在院舍或急重症环境中，案主常被指定由一名责任护士负责其长期的全面健康管理，负责与团队成员及家属保持全方位联络。在急性照护环境中，责任护士虽然不一定事必躬亲地处理每项基础擦洗，但负责全面监督一线护理人员（包括支持工作者）的实际操作质量。 4. 非护士专业人员的跨界督导：在某些特殊岗位上，支持工作者的主管可能并非护士。例如在养老院康乐文娱部，支持工作者可能直接向康乐治疗师（Recreational Therapist）汇报；在社区环境中，督导人员可能是注册社会工作者、物理治疗师或其他专业医护人员。 5. 案主本人直接雇佣与自主督导：一部分支持工作者是由案主本人（如高位截瘫但智力清晰的年轻残疾人）直接出资雇佣并由案主自行管理的私人护理员。在此类情形下，支持工作者更必须保持高度的法律警惕性，清醒界定本省限制未受监管人员执行的技术性医疗禁区，严防盲从案主要求而触犯法律底线。

    第九节：展现专业素养、仪表与人际界限 (Pages 14 - 16)

    什么是真正的专业素养？(Being a Professional & Positive Attitude)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文Professionalism is an approach to work that demonstrates respect for others, commitment, competence, and appropriate behaviour. Being cheerful and friendly, keeping work schedules, performing tasks competently, and being helpful are all part of a professional approach. To be a true professional, you must demonstrate the following:

    - Positive attitude: You need to show a good attitude about your job. The work you do is very important. People rely on you to give efficient care and support. You need to believe that you and your work are valuable and show that you enjoy your work. Be enthusiastic, considerate, and courteous.

    CN 译文专业素养（Professionalism）是一种在职业活动中充分展现对他人尊重、高度敬业承诺、精湛操作胜任力以及端庄合宜言行举止的工作方式与精神境界。保持乐观开朗与亲切友善、严格守时遵守排班、精益求精胜任操作任务、乐于助人并展现团队协作，皆是专业职业素养的具体体现。要成为一名名副其实的专业人士，你必须在工作中践行以下准则： - 积极阳光的职业态度（Positive attitude）：你必须对自己的本职工作展现出发自内心的积极态度。你所从事的支持工作对他人生命意义非凡，长者与病患完全依赖你所提供的高效照护与心理支撑。你必须坚信自己与自己的工作具有崇高的社会价值，并由衷热爱这份事业。始终保持热情、体贴与温良有礼。

    BOX 1.1 消极言辞与积极职业表达对比表 (BOX 1.1 Negative vs Positive Statements)

    原书消极言辞 (Negative Statement - 临床坚决杜绝)原书建议的积极职业表达 (Positive Statement - 倡导展现)
    "I can't. I'm too busy. Can't somebody else help?"
    “我做不了。我太忙了。难道别人就不能帮一下吗？”    		"I will make sure I do it."
    “我一定会把这件事做好并跟进到底。”
    "I didn't do it."
    “那不是我干的 / 跟我没关系。”    		"I will do it."
    “我很乐意去处理，我这就来办。”
    "It's not my fault."
    “那不是我的错。”    		"It is my responsibility."
    “这是我的分内职责，我责无旁贷。”
    "Don't blame me."
    “别怪在我头上。”    		"I accept responsibility for that."
    “我为出现这个疏漏承担责任，并立即改进。”
    "It's not my turn. I did it yesterday."
    “今天不该轮到我，我昨天才做过。”    		"I am fine with doing that."
    “我很乐意分担这项工作，没问题。”
    "Nobody told me."
    “从来没有人通知过我。”    		"I should have looked it up."
    “我本应当主动查阅护理计划确认，我这就去核实。”
    "I work harder than anyone else."
    “我干的活比这里任何人都多！”    		"I try my best."
    “我会竭尽全力做好本职工作。”
    "No one appreciates what I do."
    “根本没有人领我的情 / 没人认可我的付出。”    		"I appreciate being recognized."
    “能够获得大家的认可我心怀感激，服务对象的安康就是对我最大的回报。”

    BOX 1.2 专业仪表规范 13 条黄金法则 (Practices for a Professional Appearance)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文A professional, appropriate appearance shows respect for the people in your care, your co-workers, and yourself. It indicates that you take your job seriously.

    BOX 1.2: Practices for a Professional Appearance - Follow your employer's dress code policies. - Wear a clean, well-fitting, modest, and wrinkle-free uniform. - Wear a name badge or photo ID, per your employer's policy. - Wear clean stockings or socks that are in good repair. - Wear comfortable and clean shoes that give you good support. Shoes should provide a safe barrier for your feet from spills and body fluids. - Ensure that your underclothing cannot be seen through your uniform. - Keep your hair away from your face and up off your collar. - Use makeup sparingly. Avoid chewing gum. - Be aware that cigarette smoke and cooking odours can be absorbed into your uniform. These odours can be unpleasant to many clients. - Do not wear perfume, cologne, or aftershave. Strong scents may cause nausea or breathing problems in some clients. - Keep fingernails clean, short, and neatly shaped. Long nails can scratch the client. Artificial nails present an infection risk to clients, so they are not appropriate for support workers in any setting. - Do not wear jewellery (even if parts of your body are pierced). Jewellery may scratch or cause injury to your client and yourself and may pose infection-control risks. It may offend some clients as well. - Depending on your agency's policies, you may be asked to cover tattoos that may offend some clients.

    CN 译文端庄、职业、得体的个人仪表，彰显出你对受助案主、医护同事以及对你自身的崇高尊重，它向外界无声传递出一个明确的信号：你无比庄重、严谨地对待自己的神圣工作。

    BOX 1.2 专业仪表 13 项实践金科玉律： 1. 严格遵守着装制度：无条件执行雇主机构关于工作制服的一切着装规定。 2. 工服洁净合身端庄：穿着干净整洁、合身得体、端庄不起褶皱的工作制服（Scrubs）。 3. 佩戴官方身份胸卡：严格依规佩戴印有个人全名及照片的正式工号胸卡。 4. 袜履洁净无破损：穿着干净、无抽丝破损、色调合宜的袜子。 5. 专业安全防护鞋：穿着舒适、防滑、支撑力强且容易消毒的专用医护鞋；鞋面必须形成坚固屏障，切实保护双脚免遭体液污物溅洒伤害。 6. 严防内衣外透：务必确保内衣颜色与轮廓绝不会透过工作制服被外界隐约透视。 7. 长发利落盘起束后：长发必须整齐盘起束于脑后离开衣领，严防头发遮面、落入伤口或餐盘。 8. 妆容清淡禁嚼口香糖：仅允许化极淡职业妆；在值班和照料长者时绝对严禁咀嚼口香糖。 9. 严防二手烟与厨房油烟：必须清醒认识到烟草烟雾与烹饪油烟极易被工服纤维吸收；这些刺激性异味会令衰弱病患极其厌恶恶心。 10. 坚决执行“绝对无香化”政策：绝对严禁喷涂任何香水、古龙水、须后水或使用浓香护肤品！浓烈香精气味会直接诱发呼吸道脆弱长者的哮喘发作、剧烈咳嗽甚至呼吸衰竭。 11. 指甲修短圆润坚决杜绝假指甲：指甲必须保持洁净、剪短磨圆，防止在翻身清洁时划破脆弱皮肤；假指甲（Artificial Nails）缝隙极易藏匿多重耐药超级细菌，是引发医院交叉感染的高危元凶，在任何照护场景下均绝对禁止留贴假指甲！ 12. 禁止佩戴任何饰品：严禁佩戴首饰与吊坠饰物（即使身体有穿孔）。饰物极易刮伤长者脆弱皮肤，易在失智长者撕扯中扯裂耳垂，形成严重感控隐患，且可能冒犯某些长者。 13. 遮盖潜在冒犯性刺青：根据机构制度，在岗期间必须通过长袖等方式妥善遮盖可能令某些长者感到不安或冒犯的文身图案。

    走廊交谈防窃听、终身进修与敢于为案主发声 (Confidentiality, Lifelong Learning & Advocacy)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文...never talk with a client about another client, even if you avoid using names. Also, avoid talking about clients, co-workers, and your employer where you can be overheard; if you need to discuss a client's care with team members, make sure that other clients, families, and visitors cannot hear you. People overhearing may think you are talking about them or their family members. This assumption can lead to misinformation and confusion, which can be distressing to those affected.

    - Lifelong learning: Equipment and techniques for caring for clients can become outdated, and it is the responsibility of all caregivers to keep current with the information that affects the care they give. To keep your knowledge up to date, attend workshops and in-services that are offered by your employer, get clarifications from your supervisor, and read up about new equipment or practices that you are uncertain about. - Advocating for the client: Support workers will often make important observations about the client or family, and these observations must be communicated to the health care team. Sharing this information is especially important if a client is unable to speak for themselves. It is also the responsibility of professionals to avoid taking shortcuts in their care, as doing so may seriously harm the client. Shortcuts include taking longer breaks, skipping tasks listed on the client's care plan, or failing to apply theory on hygiene learned in school to client practice.

    CN 译文- 走廊与公共区域绝不妄议案情：在任何时候，绝不可当着一名服务对象的面去议论另一名服务对象，哪怕你刻意隐去了姓名！此外，坚决杜绝在任何可能被旁人无意听到的场所（如病区走廊、电梯间、茶水间、餐厅）谈论案主、同事或雇主；如果确需与医护同仁交接探讨某位案主的病情，必须确保其他长者、家属及来访访客绝对听不到你们的谈话内容。路过偷听到的旁人极易误以为你们是在窃窃私语讨论他们自己或其患病的家属；这种误解与猜疑会导致严重的信息混乱、信任破裂与当事人的心理创伤。 - 终身学习与业务精进（Lifelong learning）：临床照护所使用的仪器设备与操作指南随时在更新换代，时刻掌握与自己所提供照护密切相关的最新医学资讯，是每一位护理工作者的法定义务。为了确保专业知识与时俱进，必须积极参加雇主组织的业务培训和在职工作坊（In-services），虚心向上级督导请教，并主动研读掌握自己尚不熟悉的新设备或新技术规范。 - 勇于为服务对象代言发声（Advocating for the client）：支持工作者朝夕相处，往往能最敏锐观察到案主或家属的细微心理生理异常，这些关键观察必须第一时间准确汇报给跨学科团队。当案主本身失语、认知障碍而无法为自己辩护表达时，支持工作者的代言尤为神圣。同时，恪守职业道德意味着在临床照护中绝对严禁“走捷径 / 偷工减料”（Taking shortcuts）——走捷径会给脆弱案主带来致命伤害！典型的违规走捷径行为包括：擅自延长个人休息时间、擅自省略照护计划上规定的翻身或口腔护理步骤、或在临床实操中擅自抛弃学校里严谨教授的无菌洗手与消毒感控规程。

    BOX 1.3 严守界限：将私人琐事彻底排除在执业场所之外 (BOX 1.3 Keeping Personal Matters Out of Workplace)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文BOX 1.3: Keeping Personal Matters Out of the Workplace - Make personal calls only during scheduled breaks. Use a client's phone only for urgent matters. Always ask for permission before using a client's phone. - Do not discuss your personal problems at work. - Do not let your family and friends visit you at work. - Arrange your personal appointments outside of your scheduled work hours. - Do not use your employer's supplies or equipment for personal matters. - Do not try to raise funds at work, even if the funds are for a good cause.

    - Discretion about personal matters: Discretion in support work includes keeping personal matters out of the workplace. Your role is to focus on your clients and the task at hand. Do not discuss with clients your family matters and personal problems or the problems of others (BOX 1.3). No matter how well you think you know a client, remember that your relationship must remain professional. It would be inappropriate to discuss your personal problems with your client. - Using acceptable speech and language: The way you speak at home and in casual social settings may not be appropriate for a work setting. Even if you are speaking with a co-worker, others could hear you and be offended by what you say or how you say it. Your speech and language must remain professional while you are on the job. To avoid offending clients or co-workers, never use foul, vulgar, or abusive language. Also, avoid using slang. Speak gently and clearly; never yell or shout. And never fight or argue with clients, their family members, or your co-workers.

    CN 译文BOX 1.3 执业纪律规范：将个人私事彻底排除在工作场所之外 1. 私人电话严格受限：仅允许在法定的工休排歇时间内接打私人电话。严禁使用案主家中的电话，除非发生紧急危重求救状况；即便紧急使用案主电话，亦必须事先征得案主明确许可。 2. 严禁在工作中诉苦宣泄：绝不可在工作场所与案主或同事讨论个人的婚姻矛盾、家庭琐事或经济债务危机。 3. 谢绝亲友工作探访：绝不可让家人、朋友或孩子在你的工作时间前往照护现场探望陪聊。 4. 私人就医预约安排：看牙、体检、汽车年检等一切私人事务必须安排在排班工时之外。 5. 严禁公器私用：绝对不可将雇主或案主家的消毒液、手套、洗涤耗材或打印纸张据为私用。 6. 严禁在工作场所募捐或推销：绝对严禁在工作场所向长者、家属或同事推销个人产品或搞筹款集资，哪怕出于慈善目的。

    - 对个人隐私保持职业克制（Discretion about personal matters）：专业素养要求将一切个人私事完全排除在照护现场之外。你的天职是全身心聚焦于眼前的服务对象和正在执行的护理任务。无论你自认为与某位长者多么熟稔亲密，时刻切记：你们之间是神圣严肃的医患职业关系，绝非世俗的私人密友！向患病长者倾倒你个人的家庭烦恼是极度不专业、自私且严重失德的行为。 - 文明得体的职业语言规范（Using acceptable speech and language）：你在自己家里或非正式社交场合中的随意说话方式，在医疗工作场所中是完全不适宜的。即便你只是在和同事闲聊，旁人亦极易听见并受到冒犯。在执业期间，你的言辞谈吐必须时刻展现专业修养。为了避免伤害服务对象或冒犯同事，绝对严禁使用任何粗鄙脏话、低俗词汇、侮辱性或虐待性字眼！尽量避免使用市井俚语。说话必须轻柔、温和而吐字清晰；严禁大喊大叫！在任何情形下，绝对严禁与服务对象、家属或医护同事发生言语争吵或肢体冲突！

    第十节：以服务对象为中心的照护与 DIPPS 核心原则 (Pages 16 - 17)

    以服务对象为中心的核心内涵与 DIPPS 六大基石 (Client-Centred Care & DIPPS Acronym)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文THE GOAL OF SUPPORT WORK: IMPROVED QUALITY OF LIFE THROUGH CLIENT-CENTRED CARE

    Client-centred care (also known as compassionate or person-centred care) or caring means having concern for the dignity, independence, preferences, privacy, and safety of clients and their families at all times. It is important that any communication with or care given to a client be individualized for that client. The goal of support work is to demonstrate true client-centred care by following this principle and treating clients with kindness, honesty, sensitivity, comfort, discretion, respect, and understanding. Many people who require support are coping with serious illness, disability, personal issues, or challenges, and they depend on the care given to them for survival and improved quality of life. The acronym DIPPS is a reminder of the six principles of client-centred care—Dignity, Independence, Individualized care, Preferences, Privacy, and Safety—which is the goal of support work:

    - To preserve their dignity: Dignity is the state of feeling worthy, valued, and respected. People need to feel dignified. - To live independently: Clients need to do what they can for themselves. - To have all care individualized for each client: No person is identical to another person, so care should be personalized to each client. - To express their preferences: Clients need to make choices and explain how they want to have things done. - To preserve their privacy: Clients need to know that their bodies and their affairs are treated respectfully and protected from public view (FIGURE 1.4). - To be safe from harm: Clients need to live in an environment that is as hazard free as possible. They also need to feel secure about the care provided.

    When well and able-bodied, most people take the fulfillment of the six needs listed above for granted. When they are living with disabilities or suffer serious illnesses, however, these needs may be more difficult to fulfill. Those who rely on others for personal care may worry about losing their dignity, and they may not feel free to express their wishes. For example, clients living in long-term care facilities may have to eat what is provided and socialize only at prearranged times. They may share a room with another client and find that private moments are rare. Safety concerns are serious issues for clients with illnesses or disabilities. For example, they may worry about reaching the bathroom without falling.

    Clients with illnesses or disabilities do not all have the same needs. However, most have at least some of the needs just discussed. To help you recognize clients' needs, Providing Client-Centred Care boxes throughout the text discuss the goal of support work, which is to follow the principles of client-centred or compassionate care when performing procedures with clients (BOX 1.4).

    CN 译文支持工作的终极崇高宗旨：通过“以服务对象为中心的人本关怀”全面改善生活品质！

    “以服务对象为中心的照护”（Client-centred care，亦被称为慈爱关怀 Compassionate care 或以人为本的人性化照护 Person-centred care）的核心内涵在于：在任何时候，对长者及其家属的人格尊严、自立自理能力、个性化偏好、隐私权及生命安全始终怀抱深切真诚的关切。支持工作者与案主的每一次沟通交谈、所实施的每一项具体照护操作，都必须针对该个体的独特情况实现高度的“个性化定制”（Individualized）。支持工作的终极崇高使命，就是通过秉持这一原则，以仁善、诚实、敏锐、舒适、克制、尊重与深刻理解去善待每一位长者。许多需要支持照料的人士都在苦苦抵御重病折磨、严重残障或人生重大危机；他们完全依赖我们所给予的照料来维系生存并重获生命的尊严。

    首字母缩略词 DIPPS 是时刻警醒我们践行以服务对象为中心照护的六大根本伦理原则——即尊严、自立、个性化、偏好、隐私与安全： 1. 捍卫人格尊严（Dignity）：尊严是个体感受到自身有价值、受重视与受尊敬的精神心理状态。任何人都需要活得有尊严； 2. 激发自立生活（Independence）：案主需要尽其最大可能亲手完成自己尚有能力完成的事情，绝不包办替代； 3. 全程个性化定制（Individualized care）：世界上没有任何两个人是完全相同的，因此照护措施绝不可千篇一律，必须因人而异量身定制； 4. 尊重自主偏好与选择权（Preferences）：案主拥有自主做出选择并明确表达他们希望如何被照护的绝对权利； 5. 神圣捍卫身体与生活隐私（Privacy）：案主需要切实体会到自己的身体躯干与个人私人事务受到极度尊重，严防暴露在公众视线之下（参见图 1.4）； 6. 远离伤害确保安全（Safety）：案主需要在尽可能零危险隐患的环境中生活，且对所接受的照护怀有充分的安全信任感。

    在身体健全健康时，大多数人往往将这六项基本生存需要的满足视作理所当然；然而，一旦身患残疾瘫痪或遭遇重病打击，这些基本需要的满足便会变得异常艰难。那些连擦洗如厕都不得不依赖他人协助的长者，时刻深陷在丧失人格尊严的巨大恐慌之中，他们往往不敢自主表达内心的真实意愿。例如，长住长期照护机构的老人往往只能吃配餐提供的固定食物，只能在规定时间参加活动，往往需要与陌生病友共居一室而几乎毫无私密空间。跌倒等安全顾虑更是心腹大患——许多长者每天都在恐惧自己独自下床去洗手间时会不会再次跌倒骨折。

    患病或残障的长者虽然具体需求各不相同，但绝大多数都深刻经历着上述心理困境。全书中贯穿设立的“提供以服务对象为中心的照护”（Providing Client-Centred Care）特色专栏，时刻指导学员在临床为长者执行每项操作时，必须严格贯彻落实 DIPPS 人本照护基石（参见 Box 1.4）。

    BOX 1.4 DIPPS 临床实践十项落地行动准则 (BOX 1.4 DIPPS Action Steps)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文BOX 1.4: DIPPS—Principles of Client-Centred Care, the Goal of Support Work

    When performing every procedure listed in this textbook, it is important to advocate on behalf of the client's quality of life by promoting: Dignity • Independence • Individualized Care • Preferences • Privacy • Safety

    To do so, take the following steps: - Check the client's care plan before starting any procedure to familiarize yourself with the client's personalized care plan, personal preferences, and safety considerations. - Knock before entering the client's room. - Address the person by name, and introduce yourself using your name and title. - Explain the procedure before starting and explain each step throughout the procedure. Informed clients are usually more willing to assist and less likely to react out of fear. Accordingly, they are less likely to injure themselves or their caregivers during the procedure. - Obtain the client's consent for any procedure before starting. - Ensure that all care is tailored to respect the diversity of each client. - Protect the person's rights throughout the procedure, including the person's right to privacy. - Identify the client's ability to perform the procedure and guide the client to self-assist so as to remain as independent as possible. - Handle the person gently during the procedure and observe safety guidelines at all times to minimize the risk for injury to the client or to yourself. - Follow standard practice to decrease the risk for pathogen spread.

    Critical thinking, decision making, and problem-solving are crucial to the support worker's role but are often difficult when a person is new to the job. To help you with the problem-solving process, this text includes Supporting boxes that present examples of problems faced by support workers and how the problems were solved.

    CN 译文BOX 1.4 原书操作规范：DIPPS 人本照护实践十步标准法

    在执行本教材所列出的每一项临床操作程序时，支持工作者必须始终站在维护服务对象生命品质的立场上，全力捍卫： 尊严（Dignity）• 自立（Independence）• 个性化（Individualized Care）• 偏好（Preferences）• 隐私（Privacy）• 安全（Safety）

    具体必须严格执行以下十步标准化行动程序： 1. 操作前必核对护理计划（Check Care Plan）：在开始执行任何操作前，必须首先仔细查阅案主的个性化护理计划，全面熟悉其个性化需求、个人偏好及安全注意事项； 2. 进门前必须先敲门（Knock Before Entering）：进入案主居室前务必先轻声敲门，等待应允后再进入； 3. 亲切尊称姓名并主动自报家门（Address by Name & Introduce Yourself）：使用其偏好的尊称称呼长者，主动报出自己的全名及职业头衔（如：“李奶奶您好，我是您的支持工作者王明”）； 4. 操作前及操作全过程务必详细解释（Explain the Procedure）：在开始前详尽解释要做什么，并在操作全过程中解释每一步正在做什么。获得充分知情的长者通常更乐意主动配合，且更不易因惊恐而产生抗拒抵抗反应；因此，能够极大规避长者自伤或弄伤护理员的职业暴露风险； 5. 操作前必须获得长者知情同意（Obtain Consent）：在实施任何身体接触与照护操作前，必须首先征得长者的知情同意； 6. 依从文化多样性个性化施护（Respect Diversity）：确保所有照护细节均尊重每位长者的文化、信仰及个人生活习惯的多样性； 7. 全过程捍卫人权与隐私权（Protect Rights & Privacy）：在整个操作过程中严守隐私，拉合床帘、关闭房门，操作中仅暴露正在清洗或治疗的身体部位，严防身体裸露； 8. 评估自理能力并鼓励自主参与（Promote Independence）：精准评估长者残存的自理功能，鼓励并引导长者自己动手做力所能及的动作，维系其最大限度的自立机能； 9. 轻柔操作并严守安全规范（Handle Gently & Observe Safety）：动作轻柔敏捷，时刻遵守人体工效学与安全规范，锁定轮椅与床刹，将长者与自身的损伤风险降至最低； 10. 严格执行标准感染控制规程（Follow Infection Control Practices）：严格落实手部卫生、规范佩戴手套与防护用品，彻底切断病原体交叉传播途径。

    批判性思维、临床决策与解决实际问题是支持工作者角色的核心灵魂，但对初入职场的新人而言往往充满挑战。为了帮助大家掌握科学的问题解决全流程，本教材在各章中特别设计了“临床支持实战案例”（Supporting boxes）特色专栏，生动呈现一线支持工作者所遭遇的真实伦理困境与照护难题，并剖析最佳破解之道。

    第十一节：临床批判性思维与解决问题方法 (Page 17)

    临床批判性思维与问题解决的四大决策支柱 (Critical Thinking & Four Pillars of Problem-Solving)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文CRITICAL THINKING, DECISION MAKING, AND PROBLEM-SOLVING

    Support workers think critically and make many decisions in the course of a workday. For example, they estimate the time each task will take and plan the best way to complete their work on time, taking into account the various needs of the client. Many decisions involve critical thinking in order to solve problems.

    When solving problems, consider the following: - The goal of support work: Solutions to problems should not compromise the six principles of client-centred care: dignity, independence, individualized care, preferences, privacy, and safety, or DIPPS. - The client's viewpoint: Involve clients in solving problems that concern them. Examine the problem from the client's point of view. - Scope of practice: Learn and observe the rules of your workplace. Know the limits of your role. - Supervisor's viewpoint: Decide if the problem is one that you can handle on your own or one that your supervisor should handle. Your supervisor should provide guidance about which problems you can deal with on your own.

    CN 译文临床批判性思维、临床决策与照护问题解决

    在每天的工作流程中，支持工作者无时无刻不在运用批判性思维做出大量的临床决策。例如，在兼顾服务对象多维需求的前提下，合理预估每项护理任务所需的时间跨度，并科学规划出确保在排班时间内高质量完工的最优工作路径。许多日常决策都必须依托批判性思维来解决错综复杂的现场问题。

    在面对临床难题进行问题解决与决策时，支持工作者必须立足于以下四大核心支柱进行系统考量： 1. 始终对齐支持工作的终极宗旨（The Goal of Support Work）：任何问题的解决方案，都绝不可牺牲或损害“以服务对象为中心”的 DIPPS 六大根本基石（尊严、自立、个性化、偏好、隐私与安全）。 2. 充分站在服务对象的立场视角（The Client's Viewpoint）：只要该问题涉及长者自身，就必须主动邀请长者共同参与探讨解决；必须设身处地从长者的视角与切身利益来审视审判问题。 3. 严守法定执业范围红线（Scope of Practice）：熟练掌握并严格遵守所在机构的规章制度与省级法律界限，清醒知晓自己角色的权力极限，严禁越权擅断。 4. 深刻理解上级督导的专业视角（Supervisor's Viewpoint）：清醒辨析该问题究竟是属于自己有权独立现场解决的范畴，还是属于必须立即上报交由主管护士介入决策的范畴。主管督导护士应当就哪些问题支持工作者可以自主处置、哪些问题必须立即上报提供清晰的指引规范。

    第十二节：全书章节要点回顾与批判性思维实战 (Page 18)

    核心知识要点总结 (CHAPTER REVIEW · KEY POINTS)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文CHAPTER REVIEW · KEY POINTS - The ultimate goal of support work is to improve the client's quality of life by following the principles of client-centred care (DIPPS). The support worker tends to the person's needs, which include physical needs as well as helping to relieve loneliness, providing comfort, encouraging independence, and promoting the person's self-respect. Care should always be individualized to each client. A support worker's services to people in their homes help them remain independent and continue to live with their families. Support workers make a difference in people's lives. - Support workers can work in community-based or facility-based settings. Duties will vary according to the setting. - Most of a support worker's responsibilities can be grouped into six categories: (1) personal care, (2) support for nurses and other health care providers, (3) family support, (4) social support, (5) housekeeping or home management, and (6) documenting and reporting any basic assessments, observations, and care provided in the client's record or chart. - Depending on their setting, people receiving health care and support services are known by different terms, such as patient (in a hospital), resident (in a live-in facility), or client (in the community). - The people supported can be grouped according to their problems, needs, and ages. - At this point, support workers in most provinces and territories are still considered health care providers not regulated through legislation, or unregulated care providers (UCPs). Support workers are accountable to their supervisors, employers, and clients; they do not presently have an organization or college that governs their role. Regulated care providers are health care providers licensed and regulated by the province or territory to carry out care that is within their scope of practice. - There are many members of the health care team, with the client always at the centre of care. - It is important that support workers understand their scope of practice and never act beyond their role. - In many settings, support workers are supervised by nurses or other professionals. - Professionalism includes having a positive attitude, a sense of responsibility, a professional appearance, being discreet about client information, being a lifelong learner, advocating for the client, being discreet about personal matters, and using acceptable speech and language. - While discussing the client with co-workers, support workers must use discretion at all times and honour the client's right to confidentiality. Confidentiality means respecting and guarding personal and private information about another person. - Part of being a professional, reflective practice reviews consist of a review and self-evaluation of any care provided and identification of ways to improve clinical performance. - When thinking critically, making decisions, and solving problems, support workers must consider the principles of client-centred care (DIPPS), the client's viewpoint, their scope of practice, and their supervisor's viewpoint.

    CN 译文第 1 章核心知识要点权威回顾（KEY POINTS）： 1. 支持工作的终极使命：支持工作的根本宗旨在于践行 DIPPS 六大人本原则以提升长者的生命品质。支持工作者全方位满足长者的身心需求，排遣孤独、倾注温情抚慰、激发自主自立、维护人格尊严。照护必须全程个性化。入户服务能赋能长者在熟悉的家中自立生存，支持工作者真正改变了他人的生命轨迹。 2. 执业场所多样性：支持工作者既可执业于社区环境亦可执业于院舍机构，具体职责随场所特性动态调整。 3. 六大核心职责体系：(1) 个人生活照料；(2) 协助护士及其他医护人员；(3) 家庭系统支持；(4) 社交人际支持；(5) 家务管理；(6) 基础评估、观察所见与照护记录书面汇报。 4. 受助人群的三大称谓：医院中称为患者（Patient），全寄宿机构中称为院舍长者（Resident），社区家庭中称为案主（Client），通称为服务对象。 5. 受助人群九大分类：可依据健康问题、多维需求与年龄跨度清晰归纳为九大核心类别。 6. 未受监管照护者（UCPs）法定属性：目前在绝大多数省份支持工作者属于 UCPs，向督导、雇主及案主问责，暂无专门法定行业公会直接发牌管理；而受监管专业人员（如护士、医生）由省级专业公会直接发牌规制。 7. 以案主为中心的跨学科团队：医疗团队汇聚多学科专业力量，但服务对象始终处于整个照护体系的正中央核心。 8. 执业范围红线原则：深刻领悟所在省份教育标准与雇主制度所界定的执业范围（Scope of Practice），绝不擅自越权。 9. 多层级督导机制：在绝大多数场景下，支持工作者接受注册护士（RN）或实用护士（RPN/LPN）等专科人员的临床督导。 10. 专业素养八大支柱：专业素养涵盖积极阳光态度、高度责任心、专业规范仪表、案主信息保密审慎、终身学习、敢于为长者发声、将个人私事完全排除于职场外、文明合宜的语言谈吐。 11. 信息隐私保密权（Confidentiality）：与同事探讨案情时必须严守保密义务，尊重并全力捍卫服务对象的一切个人健康与私人隐私信息。 12. 反思性执业回顾（Reflective Practice Review）：作为专业素养的核心，从业者必须定期对自身实施的各项照护进行批判性自省评估，确保持续精进临床操作品质。 13. 临床决策四大维度：在运用批判性思维解决问题时，必须综合平衡 DIPPS 人本照护宗旨、案主切身视角、法定执业范围以及上级督导的专业指引。

    临床实践中的批判性思维案例情境 (CRITICAL THINKING IN PRACTICE) (Page 18)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文CRITICAL THINKING IN PRACTICE In the clinical setting, a resident tells you that she has been getting out of bed on her own at night, even though she was instructed to ask for assistance whenever ambulating. She has fallen several times in the past and fractured her wrist the last time she fell. How will you respond? What will you do? As a student, what rules must you follow to protect patients and residents?

    【题目翻译与临床决策指引】 在临床长照机构实习中，一位长期入住的长者悄悄告诉你：她最近每天夜间都自己偷偷下床走动去洗手间，尽管医护团队三令五申要求她每次离床走动前必须按呼叫铃寻求人员协助。该长者过去曾多次发生严重跌倒，在上一次跌倒中甚至导致了手腕粉碎性骨折。老人家哀求你替她保守这个“秘密”，不要告诉护士。 - 你应当如何与长者沟通回应？ - 你必须立刻采取什么临床行动？ - 作为一名实习学员或在职支持工作者，你必须严格遵守哪些安全铁律来全力保护病患与长者的生命安全？

    【权威临床决策精解与实战行动准则】 1. 治疗性沟通与共情劝导：切勿用严厉生硬的口吻训斥长者。首先以温和共情的态度肯定其渴望自主自立的心情，但必须耐心向其解释：既往多次跌倒且伴有严重骨折史，夜间视线昏暗、突发体位性低血压极易再次发生致命跌倒，可能导致严重的股骨颈骨折、长期卧床、完全丧失自理能力甚至危及生命。 2. 果断干预与团队安全闭环汇报：离开房间前立即落实跌倒防范措施：确保病床摇降至最低安全高度，将呼叫铃手柄放置在长者健侧唾手可及之处，清理床旁与地面一切障碍物，开启夜间照明地灯。最为关键的一步：支持工作者绝不可为了向长者盲目承诺而隐瞒安全隐患，必须在离开房间后第一时间立即向当班主管督导护士（Supervising Nurse）如实汇报该高危隐患！以便跨学科团队紧急重新评估长者的跌倒风险指数，调整防跌倒干预计划（如安装离床重力感应报警垫、提高夜间巡视频率等）。 3. 实习学生守则与伦理底线：生命安全（Safety）在支持工作一切价值排序中始终位列最高优先级！支持工作者绝不能隐瞒长者的高危行动。对案主生命安全和严重躯体伤害的预防，具有绝对的压倒性，绝对优先于长者个人“不愿声张”的盲目要求。
    Chapter 1 Self-Test & Review Questions · Pages 18–19 12 Authentic Clinical Questions
    KNOWLEDGE CHECK · INTERACTIVE PRACTICE

    第 1 章：角色与职责全真自测复习题与详尽临床精解

    原版 12 道标准执业自测题（原书第 18–19 页 · 12 道精解）。点击选项可即时测试并验证对错，点击“查看权威答案与临床精解”即可展开详尽的加拿大 PSW 临床实操解析与知识点溯源。

    QUESTION 01 待作答
    1. Activities of daily living (ADLs) are:
    A Social and recreational activities
    B Activities that support workers perform to prevent injuries
    C Physical exercises that people perform daily to keep themselves fit
    D Self-care activities that people perform daily to remain independent and to function in society
    标准权威答案： 选项
    临床实操解析与知识点溯源：
    日常生活活动（ADLs）特指维持个体基本生存和自立生活不可或缺的日常自我照护活动（Self-care activities），包括进食、穿衣、沐浴、个人修饰整理、如厕及床上/轮椅转移。选项 A 属于社交娱乐；选项 B 指人体工效学与职业安全防护；选项 C 指日常健身锻炼，均不符合 ADLs 的医学定义。

    ---
    QUESTION 02 待作答
    2. Which of the following is a way in which support workers assist nurses or other health care team members?
    A Assess the client's needs
    B Order range-of-motion exercises
    C Witness legal signatures on permission forms
    D Report changes in the client's behaviour or health
    标准权威答案： 选项
    临床实操解析与知识点溯源：
    支持工作者是主管督导护士在临床第一线的“眼睛和耳朵”（Eyes and ears）。由于陪伴时间最长，支持工作者最重要的法定协助职责就是敏锐观察案主的精神状态、情绪反应及身体体征，并将任何细微的病情变化第一时间汇报给责任护士。选项 A（评估诊断）、选项 B（开具医疗康复医嘱）和选项 C（见证知情同意法律文书）均属于注册专业医护人员的法定专属执业范围，支持工作者严禁越权执行。

    ---
    QUESTION 03 待作答
    3. Resident is a term used to describe a person who is receiving care at:
    A Home
    B A long-term care facility
    C An outpatient clinic
    D A hospital
    标准权威答案： 选项
    临床实操解析与知识点溯源：
    在加拿大医疗体系中，术语使用具有严格的法定场所属性：在全寄宿型长期照护机构（Long-term care facility）中长住并接受生活与护理照料的老人被称为“Resident”；在综合医院（Hospital）接受急危重症救治的被称为“Patient”；在家庭与社区（Home / Community）接受上门照护的被称为“Client”。

    ---
    QUESTION 04 待作答
    4. The main focus of the health care team is to:
    A See as many clients as possible
    B Provide quality care for the client
    C Complete assigned tasks as quickly as possible
    D Find a cure for the client's illness or condition
    标准权威答案： 选项
    临床实操解析与知识点溯源：
    跨学科医疗卫生照护团队的根本宗旨是“以服务对象为中心”（Client-centred care），其凝聚所有专业人员共同协作的核心目标始终是为案主提供安全、有尊严且高质量的全面照护（Provide quality care）。选项 A 追求走量、选项 C 忙于赶工走捷径，均严重违背临床伦理；选项 D 虽然美好，但长期照护中的大多数慢性病、重度失智症及衰老并不存在所谓彻底“治愈”的可能，支持工作的焦点在于改善生活品质。

    ---
    QUESTION 05 待作答
    5. Support workers are:
    A Unregulated care providers
    B Licensed health care workers
    C Members of a professional college
    D Members of a regulatory body
    标准权威答案： 选项
    临床实操解析与知识点溯源：
    在现阶段加拿大绝大多数省份和地区，支持工作者均被归类为“未受监管照护提供者”（Unregulated Care Providers, UCPs）。他们并不由省级专门法案发牌监管，不设立直接管理其执业准入的专业行业公会（Professional College）。支持工作者在督导护士或雇主机构的管理下提供支持服务，向其雇主和主管督导承担职业责任。

    ---
    QUESTION 06 待作答
    6. Scope of practice means:
    A The tasks that are assigned by your supervisor
    B The tasks that a client asks you to perform
    C The effort you put into performing a task or procedure
    D The limits of your role to prevent client harm
    标准权威答案： 选项
    临床实操解析与知识点溯源：
    执业范围（Scope of Practice）特指由省级教育培训标准、雇主管理规章制度以及相关法律共同界定的，支持工作者被法律允许执行与严禁涉足的职业技能与行为边界。其根本立法目的在于通过给无照人员设立清晰的权限边界，彻底防范因越权技术操作而致案主遭受伤害（Prevent client harm）。主管护士分派的任务或案主的要求绝不能凌驾于法定执业范围之上。

    ---
    QUESTION 07 待作答
    7. Professionalism is:
    A A term used to describe workplace settings
    B An approach to work that demonstrates respect for others, commitment, competence, and appropriate behaviour
    C A commitment made by regulated professionals
    D Another term for confidentiality
    标准权威答案： 选项
    临床实操解析与知识点溯源：
    教材明确指出，专业素养（Professionalism）是一种综合性的职业态度与工作方式，其核心基石包括四大要素：展现对他人发自内心的尊重（Respect for others）、高度敬业尽责的职业承诺（Commitment）、精益求精胜任操作的过硬专业能力（Competence）以及在任何场合下均端庄得体的言行举止（Appropriate behaviour）。它绝非受监管人员的专利，支持工作者同样必须时刻践行高水准的专业素养。

    ---
    QUESTION 08 待作答
    8. Which of the following is true?
    A You can use a client's phone to make personal calls.
    B Friends can visit you at work.
    C You must follow your employer's dress code policies.
    D Sharing your personal problems with a client shows compassion.
    标准权威答案： 选项
    临床实操解析与知识点溯源：
    根据原书 Box 1.2 及 Box 1.3 职业界限守则：选项 C 严格遵守雇主着装规范（Follow employer's dress code）是支持工作者最基本的职业纪律要求。选项 A 严禁使用案主电话（除非遇紧急抢救并征得许可）；选项 B 严禁亲友在工作时间探访；选项 D 向案主倾倒个人生活私事属于严重违背职业界限（Professional boundaries）的失德行为，绝非慈爱关怀。

    ---
    QUESTION 09 待作答
    9. In a long-term care facility, the client's information should be shared among:
    A Health care team members involved in the client's care
    B Health care team members and friends who visit the client
    C Family and friends of the client
    D All staff members at the facility
    标准权威答案： 选项
    临床实操解析与知识点溯源：
    根据加拿大最高级别健康信息隐私法律（如 PHIPA）与医疗伦理，长者的健康档案与个人隐私信息受法律绝对保护。只有拥有“法定知情必要”（Need-to-know）、直接参与该案主日常照料的医疗团队责任成员，才能在履职所需的最低限度内共享信息。探访的朋友（选项 B）、非授权亲友（选项 C）以及未直接参与照护的其他非责任员工（选项 D，如行政后勤人员）均无权获取该私密医疗信息。

    ---
    QUESTION 10 待作答
    10. Critical thinking means:
    A Keeping one's feelings to oneself
    B Approaching your work with enthusiasm
    C Being critical when thinking or speaking of others
    D The ability to think clearly and logically, using reflection and reason
    标准权威答案： 选项
    临床实操解析与知识点溯源：
    教材在第 13 页与第 17 页给出了严谨的学术定义：批判性思维（Critical Thinking）是指立足于从实践经验、敏锐观察或正规教育中获取的专业知识，运用反思与理性逻辑（Reflection and reason）进行清晰、严密分析与科学推理的心智技能。选项 C 混淆了日常俗语中“挑剔批评”的贬义，与医学临床批判性思维南辕北辙。

    ---
    QUESTION 11 待作答
    11. The acronym DIPPS stands for:
    A Disability, independence, preferences, policies, privacy, sympathy
    B Dignity, independence, individualized care, preferences, privacy, safety
    C Discretion, individuality, pity, privacy, support
    D Disability, individuality, pity, privacy, scope of practice
    标准权威答案： 选项
    临床实操解析与知识点溯源：
    根据原书第 16 页最新第 5 版权威定义，DIPPS 是以服务对象为中心照护（Client-centred care）的六大支柱，每个字母分别代表：
    - **D** = Dignity（尊严）
    - **I** = Independence（自立）
    - **I** = Individualized care（个性化定制照护）
    - **P** = Preferences（自主偏好）
    - **P** = Privacy（隐私保密）
    - **S** = Safety（人身安全）。
    任何包含 Sympathy（同情/怜悯）、Pity（居高临下的可怜）或 Disability 的选项均彻底违背 DIPPS 的人本平等照护宗旨。

    ---
    QUESTION 12 待作答
    12. Which statement is false? When solving problems, you should:
    A Consider your scope of practice
    B Consider the principles of client-centred care
    C Discuss the problem with the client
    D Not involve the client to prevent causing more problems
    标准权威答案： 选项
    临床实操解析与知识点溯源：
    本题考查的是反向否定选择题（Which statement is FALSE）。根据原书第 17 页临床决策四大要素之“案主立场（The client's viewpoint）”明确要求：只要该问题涉及案主切身利益，就必须全力鼓励并吸纳案主共同参与讨论解决；擅自剥夺案主的知情权与参与权、将案主排除在外（选项 D），严重践踏了案主的自主权与自尊心，因而是彻底错误且违背伦理的行为，符合题干所要求的选出错误项。

    Sorrentino's Canadian Textbook for the Support Worker (5th Edition)

    Chapter 2: The Canadian Health Care System

    第 2 章：加拿大医疗卫生保健体系（原版逐字逐句 14 页全文典藏版）

    【开篇导读与学习目标】Chapter 2 Opener & Learning Objectives (Page 20)

    学习目标 (Objectives)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文After reading this chapter, the learner should be able to do the following: • Describe medicare and how it has evolved. • Identify the federal, provincial, and territorial roles in the Canadian health care system. • Summarize the principles of medicare described in the Canada Health Act. • Differentiate among primary, secondary, and tertiary health care deliveries. • Identify how the focus of the Canadian health care system is shifting to home care. • Explain why health promotion and disease prevention are important functions of the Canadian health care system. • Recognize the emerging importance of home care and the support worker's role in providing some of these services.

    CN 译文完成本章研读后，学习者应当能够达成以下核心专业能力考核目标： • 阐述加拿大公费全民医疗保险体系（Medicare）的本质特征及其历史演进脉络。 • 明确界定加拿大联邦政府、各省及地区政府在国家医疗卫生体系中各自承担的法定职能与分工。 • 提炼并熟练掌握《加拿大健康法案》（Canada Health Act）所确立的全民医疗五大核心基石原则。 • 准确鉴别与区分初级、二级与三级医疗卫生照护服务（Primary, Secondary, and Tertiary Care）的执业场景与临床内涵。 • 深入理解并辨识加拿大医疗卫生服务重心从传统医院机构向社区居家照护（Home Care）深刻战略转移的时代背景与动因。 • 深入阐释“健康促进”（Health Promotion）与“疾病预防”（Disease Prevention）为何成为现代加拿大医疗卫生体系不可或缺的核心功能。 • 深刻认识居家照护日益凸显的战略地位，以及支持工作者在向案主提供这些基础支持服务中所发挥的关键前沿角色。

    【核心专业术语表】Chapter 2 Key Terms (Page 21)

    accessibility /əkˌsɛsəˈbɪləti/
    可及性 / 获得合理照护原则

    《加拿大健康法案》五大基石原则之一。明确规定所有居民必须在不受任何额外财务阻碍或歧视的前提下，公平、合理地获得受保医疗服务。 (p. 22)

    benefits /ˈbɛnɪfɪts/
    补充医疗保险福利

    通过所缴纳的保险保费或雇主保险所提供的特定医疗补助服务。例如由牙医执行且消费者无需自付额外费用的牙科医疗项目。 (p. 30)

    Canada Health Act (1984) /ˈkænədə hɛlθ ækt/
    《加拿大健康法案》 (1984)

    奠定加拿大全民公费医疗的联邦核心根本大法。界定了受保医疗服务的法定范围，并确立了各省/地区为获取联邦全额医疗现金拨款所必须严格遵守的五大准则（全面性、普遍性、便携性、可及性与公立管理）。 (p. 21)

    comprehensiveness /ˌkɒmprɪˈhɛnsɪvnəs/
    全面性原则

    《加拿大健康法案》五大基石原则之一。明确规定该保险计划必须全面覆盖医院内所有医学必需服务，以及医师与口腔颌面外科医生所提供的必需治疗。 (p. 22)

    disease prevention /dɪˈziːz prɪˈvɛnʃən/
    疾病预防

    旨在阻止疾病、身体机能损伤或意外创伤发生的一系列前置干预策略与公共卫生行动。 (p. 31)

    health promotion /hɛlθ prəˈmoʊʃən/
    健康促进

    通过为个人、群体和整个社区提供关键卫生科普知识与行动工具，赋权大众做出明智健康抉择，从而全面增进与维护人群健康福祉的战略举措。 (p. 31)

    home care /hoʊm kɛər/
    居家照护 / 上门支持服务

    在服务对象个人居住住所（包括私人家庭、辅助生活居所或持牌照护住所）内所提供的医疗卫生与生活支持服务。 (p. 27)

    medicare /ˈmɛdɪˌkɛər/
    加拿大全民公费医疗系统

    加拿大的全国性医疗卫生保险体系，通过公共税收全额资助，为所有永久居民和公民提供免自费的医学必需医疗服务。 (p. 21)

    portability /ˌpɔːrtəˈbɪləti/
    可携带性 / 跨省便携原则

    《加拿大健康法案》五大基石原则之一。确保居民在跨省求学、搬迁、换工作、境内旅游或在海外短期停留期间，仍能持续享受原居住省份所提供的医疗保障。 (p. 21)

    primary health care delivery /ˈpraɪmɛri hɛlθ kɛər/
    初级卫生保健服务

    居民与医疗保健体系发生的第一道接触点（如家庭医生门诊、执业执照护士门诊、社区诊所或远程健康热线），聚焦于健康促进与早期筛查。 (p. 25)

    public administration /ˈpʌblɪk ədˌmɪnɪˈstreɪʃən/
    公立非营利性管理原则

    《加拿大健康法案》五大基石原则之一。明确规定各省的公费医疗保险计划必须由非营利性的公共法定机构全权行政主管，并向公众及立法机关完全公开负责。 (p. 22)

    quaternary care /ˈkwɑːtərˌnɛri kɛər/
    四级前沿超特精专医疗

    三级医疗（Tertiary Care）的极度专精延伸层级，包含在顶级科研教学医院开展的高难度实验性药物疗法或开创性尖端复杂外科手术。 (p. 26)

    secondary health care delivery /ˈsɛkənˌdɛri hɛlθ kɛər/
    二级专业专科医疗服务

    医疗照护的中级层级，涵盖专业评估、确诊与专科治疗，通常在具备精密医疗仪器设备和专业检验病理实验室的综合医院由专科医师执行。 (p. 26)

    telehealth /ˈtɛliˌhɛlθ/
    远程医疗健康呼叫中心

    设立专用医疗健康电话呼叫中心网络（如安省 Telehealth Ontario / 811 热线），由注册护士 24 小时向致电公众提供专业的健康分诊咨询与就医指引。 (p. 26)

    tertiary health care delivery /ˈtɜːrʃiˌɛri hɛlθ kɛər/
    三级高阶专精重症医疗

    在大型学术研究型教学医院所提供的高难度、高度技术密集型专精医疗服务（如重症监护、复杂心胸外科及神经外科）。 (p. 26)

    traditional healing /trəˈdɪʃənl ˈhiːlɪŋ/
    原住民传统医学与身心疗愈

    融入加拿大原住民传统世界观的身心疗愈知识与实践体系，运用神圣仪式、动植物与矿物草药、能量疗法或身体推拿手法实现全人平衡。 (p. 24)

    universality /ˌjuːnɪvɜːrˈsæləti/
    全民普遍受保原则

    《加拿大健康法案》五大基石原则之一。规定各省全体永久居民在完全相同的条款与条件下，人人享有均等的受保基础医疗卫生服务权利，严禁阶层差异。 (p. 22)

    英文核心术语 (Key Term)音标 (Phonetic)权威中文译名原版学术定义与临床内涵 (Definition & Clinical Context)
    accessibility/əkˌsɛsəˈbɪləti/可及性 / 获得合理照护原则《加拿大健康法案》五大基石原则之一。明确规定所有居民必须在不受任何额外财务阻碍或歧视的前提下，公平、合理地获得受保医疗服务。 (p. 22)
    benefits/ˈbɛnɪfɪts/补充医疗保险福利通过所缴纳的保险保费或雇主保险所提供的特定医疗补助服务。例如由牙医执行且消费者无需自付额外费用的牙科医疗项目。 (p. 30)

    第一节：加拿大现行医疗体制总览与 Medicare 核心理念 (Pages 21 - 22)

    崇高信念与全民公费医疗系统概述 (Belief in Universal Access & Medicare)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文Most Canadians believe that quality health care should be available to all Canadian citizens, regardless of their ability to pay for services. Canada's national health insurance system, known as medicare, which is envied throughout the world, was developed to achieve this goal. Medicare uses provincial or territorial taxes and federal taxes to pay for all medically necessary health services for all permanent residents. As members of Canada's changing health care system, support workers have an increasingly important role to play.

    CN 译文绝大多数加拿大人坚信：每一位公民无论个人贫富、无论是否具备即时支付能力，都应当享有获得高品质医疗卫生服务的神圣权利。享誉全球并令世人称羡的加拿大国家医疗保险体系——通称“全民医疗体系”（Medicare）——正是为了践行这一崇高愿景而建立。Medicare 汇集各省/地区税收与联邦政府税收，共同出资全额支付所有永久居民在医学上所必需的一切医疗服务费用。作为身处这一不断变革与重塑的医疗卫生系统中的专业从业者，支持工作者（PSW / HCA）所肩负的使命与角色正变得前所未有的关键与崇高。

    联邦与省/地区交织的医疗管理架构 (An Interlocking Public System)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文CANADA'S CURRENT HEALTH CARE SYSTEM

    Canada's publicly funded health care system is best described as an interlocking set of health insurance plans, with the federal government and the 10 provincial and 3 territorial governments sharing responsibilities within Canada's health care system. The provincial and territorial governments fund health care services with assistance from the federal government. To receive their full share of federal funding for health care, provincial and territorial medicare plans must meet five criteria—comprehensiveness, universality, portability, accessibility, and public administration (see BOX 2.1 on page 22)—that are provided in the federal government's Canada Health Act.

    Medicare provides access to universal, comprehensive coverage for hospital and physician services that are medically necessary. These services are administered and delivered by the provincial and territorial governments to Canadian residents with no payment necessary upon delivery of the service. However, each province or territory is responsible for developing its own health care policies, so some policy inconsistencies may exist from one province to another.

    CN 译文加拿大现行医疗卫生保健体系架构

    加拿大由公共税收资助的医疗卫生保健体系，最贴切的描述是一个“环环相扣、相互咬合的医疗保险网络”：由加拿大联邦政府与 10 个省政府、3 个地区政府共同分工协作、分级承担管理权责。各省及地区政府在联邦政府财政专项转移支付（Canada Health Transfer, CHT）的鼎力协助下，直接规划、资助并组织实施境内的医疗照护服务。为了有资格全额申领联邦每年拨付的巨额医疗专款，各省与地区的医保计划必须无条件恪守联邦《加拿大健康法案》中所明文规定的五大核心准则：全面性、普遍性、便携性、可及性与公立管理（详见第 22 页 BOX 2.1）。

    Medicare 为全加居民提供了全面覆盖所有“医学必需”的医院住院与执业医师诊疗服务的普惠保障。这些服务由各省和地区政府直接管理运营并交付到千家万户手中，受助居民在接受诊断治疗的当下无需掏出钱包支付任何医疗费用（即现场零自付费用）。然而，由于每个省份与地区拥有制定本辖区具体卫生行政政策的宪法自主权，因此在不同省份之间，具体的资助细节和边缘服务覆盖范围存在一定程度的地区差异与政策不一致。

    第二节：《加拿大健康法案》五大基石原则 (Page 22)

    BOX 2.1 《加拿大健康法案》规定的全民医疗五大准则 (The Five Principles of Medicare)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文BOX 2.1: The Principles of Medicare, as Listed in the Canada Health Act (1984)

    1. Comprehensiveness. The insurance plan must pay for all medically necessary services. In a hospital, all necessary drugs, supplies, and diagnostic tests are covered. A range of necessary services provided outside a hospital are also covered. 2. Universality. Every permanent resident of a province or territory is entitled to receive the insured health care services provided by the plan, on similar terms and conditions. 3. Portability. People can keep their health care coverage even if they are unemployed, change jobs, relocate between provinces and territories, or travel within Canada or abroad. However, if they are moving, it is their responsibility to inform their province or territory and to register with their new province or territory. During the transition, they will be covered by their previous jurisdiction's health coverage for up to 3 months (see Supporting Mr. Wloch: An Example of Canada's Health Insurance Portability box on page 31). 4. Accessibility. People can receive medically necessary services regardless of their income, age, health status, gender, or geographical location. Additional charges (privatization) for publicly insured services are not permitted. 5. Public administration. The insurance plan must be run by a public organization on a nonprofit basis. The public organization must be accountable to the citizens and the government of the province or territory.

    CN 译文BOX 2.1 专栏文献：《加拿大健康法案》（1984年）确立的全民医疗五大根本基石原则

    1. 全面性原则（Comprehensiveness）：各省医保计划必须全额资助并支付所有医学上必需的医疗服务。在医院住院期间，所有必需的处方药品、医用消耗器材以及生化影像诊断检查全部受保；医院之外由执业医生提供的一系列必需医疗诊治亦在受保之列。 2. 全民普遍受保原则（Universality）：所在省份或地区的每一位合法永久居民，均在完全相同且公平的法定条款与条件下，享有获得该医保计划所覆盖的所有受保医疗服务的合法权利。 3. 跨省便携与延续性原则（Portability）：居民在失业、更换工作单位、跨省或跨地区永久搬迁、境内跨省旅行或短期赴海外旅行期间，其享有的公费医疗保障资格绝不因此中断或失效。但是，若发生跨省永久搬迁，居民有法定义务主动通知原籍省份卫生厅，并及时向新迁入省份的卫生管理部门登记建档；在过渡期内，原居住省份将继续为其提供长达 3 个月的过渡医疗保障（参见第 31 页弗洛赫先生跨省迁徙案例）。 4. 平等合理可及性原则（Accessibility）：全体居民在获得医学必需的诊疗服务时，绝不受其个人经济收入、年龄阶段、既往健康状况、生理性别或所处偏远地理位置的限制或歧视。绝对严禁针对公费受保项目向病患额外收取任何“加急费”、“挂号自费”或变相私有化费用！ 5. 公立非营利性统一管理原则（Public Administration）：医保计划必须由非营利性的公共法定管理机构全权统一运营管理。该公立机构必须保持高度财政与运营透明，直接向全省广大纳税公民以及省立法议会全面负责。

    第三节：加拿大医疗体制百年演进史与“最伟大的加拿大人” (Pages 22 - 23)

    早期自费苦难、大萧条与互助萌芽 (Early Challenges & The Great Depression)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文The Evolution of Canada's Health Care System

    In the first part of the twentieth century, individuals in Canada were expected to pay the entire amount of their doctors' bills and hospital fees. Often, there were no "set fees" for services; that is, for the same treatment, a physician could charge one patient a certain amount and another patient an entirely different fee, depending on what the physician thought the patient could afford to pay. Those who could not afford to pay sometimes had to access charity through community agencies such as the Victorian Order of Nurses, the Red Cross, and local churches (FIGURE 2.1), and some people just went without health care altogether.

    The Great Depression of the 1930s had a dramatic effect on Canada's health care system. Families struggled to feed, clothe, and house their members and could not possibly pay their medical bills. A serious illness or stay in a hospital caused financial disaster for many. The cost of care prevented others from even seeking medical treatment, so those who had illnesses and disabilities would instead depend on family members and neighbours to provide care. These hardships inspired Canadians to create a prepaid medical and hospitalization insurance plan.

    CN 译文加拿大全民公费医疗体制的百年演进史

    在 20 世纪前叶，加拿大社会的医疗完全遵循纯市场自费模式，个人必须自行承担全部的诊所看诊账单与住院费用。彼时医疗收费往往没有统一的标准规约：对于完全相同的医疗操作，医生往往凭借自己对病患家庭经济状况的直观判断，向富有者索取高昂费用，而向贫困者收取较少费用。那些完全无力负担诊费的极度贫困底层，只能向加拿大维多利亚护士会（VON）、红十字会以及地方教会等民间慈善机构乞求施舍救助（参见图 2.1），甚至有大量贫民在患病后完全得不到任何医疗救治而悲惨离世。

    20 世纪 30 年代的“经济大萧条”（The Great Depression）给加拿大医疗卫生体系带来了极其沉重而深刻的剧烈震荡。成千上万的家庭在温饱与住所边缘苦苦挣扎，根本无力支付哪怕一张微薄的医药账单。突如其来的一场严重疾病或一次紧急住院，往往直接导致整个家庭倾家荡产、沦为赤贫。沉重的高昂费用逼得许多患病长者与残疾人士不敢涉足诊所一步，只能完全依赖家属与邻里的简陋看护勉强度日。正是在这场深重的人道灾难与生活苦难中，加拿大人民深刻觉醒，萌生了共同出资构建“预付制全民住院与医疗保险制度”的坚定历史信念。

    萨斯喀彻温省的破冰革新与汤米·道格拉斯 (Tommy Douglas & The Birth of Medicare)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文In 1947, under Premier Tommy Douglas, Saskatchewan became the first province to introduce a public insurance plan that covered the costs of hospital services (BOX 2.2). By 1972, all provinces and territories had extended their insurance plans to also cover medical services provided outside hospitals. Again, provincial and territorial governments and the federal government shared the health care expenses roughly equally. Modern medicare began that year, giving all permanent residents free access to the same quality of hospital and medical care, regardless of their personal financial status.

    BOX 2.2: Tommy Douglas, the "Greatest Canadian" In the spring of 2004, the Canadian Broadcasting Corporation (CBC) invited Canadians to submit their nominations for the "Greatest Canadian" of all time. Canadians responded with thousands of worthy suggestions. After 6 weeks of voting, Canadians chose Tommy Douglas. You might be wondering who he was and why Canadians are grateful for his contributions to this country.

    Thomas Clement Douglas (1904–1986) reorganized Saskatchewan's post-war employment, social, and public health policies. Douglas's reforms became his legacy, as they formed the basis for Canada's social and health care policies, which continue to exist today. For example, as Canada's health minister (1944–1948), he took the first steps toward what we now call medicare, by: • Establishing a minimum wage and ensuring a maximum 44-hour workweek and paid 2-week vacation leave, rights some workers take for granted today • Overseeing increases in old-age pensions and mother's allowance • Legislating that medical and hospital benefits be given to welfare recipients at no out-of-pocket cost to them at the time of care

    These are just a few of the many contributions that Tommy Douglas made. Without him, Canada would be a very different country today. We can all be grateful for the role he played in making Canada the country that it is.

    CN 译文1947 年，在省长汤米·道格拉斯（Tommy Douglas）的无畏魄力推动下，萨斯喀彻温省（Saskatchewan）敢为人先，成为全加拿大乃至北美大陆第一个成功推出公费住院医疗保险计划的省份（详见 BOX 2.2）。截至 1972 年，加拿大所有省份和地区均已顺应时代潮流，全面将公立医保覆盖范围从医院内部拓展至医院外部的门诊就医。在此阶段，各省/地区政府与联邦政府大致均摊了全部医疗公共开支。自此，现代意义上的加拿大全民公费医疗体制宣告全面确立——不论个人贫富贵贱，所有永久居民均平等享有免费获得同等优质医院与诊疗救护的神圣权利。

    BOX 2.2 历史风云人物专栏：汤米·道格拉斯 —— “最伟大的加拿大人” 在 2004 年春季，加拿大广播公司（CBC）面向全国公众发起轰动一时的海选征集，推选加拿大建国以来“最伟大的加拿大人”（The Greatest Canadian）。全加民众热烈响应，提交了数万名卓越候选人。历经长达 6 周的全国性全民公投，加拿大人民最终将这一至高无上的历史荣耀桂冠授予了汤米·道格拉斯。学员可能会好奇：这位伟人究竟是谁？为何数代加拿大人至今仍对他怀揣如此无尽的感激？

    托马斯·克莱门特·道格拉斯（Thomas Clement Douglas，1904–1986）在二战后主政萨省期间，大刀阔斧重构了该省的就业、社会福利与公共卫生政策。他的开创性立法改革成为了他留给国家最宝贵的政治遗产，直接奠定了现代加拿大社会福利与全民医疗制度的基石： • 创立最低法定工资保障制度，确立法宝级的每周最长 44 小时工作制及两周带薪年假权利——这些在当今被视作理所当然的劳工权益，在当年皆由他披荆斩棘率先确立； • 统筹推动提高长者养老金（Old-Age Pensions）及单身母亲扶助津贴； • 颁布历史性法令，强制规定向社会救助领取者提供完全免费的医疗和住院保障，在就医时严禁其自掏腰包。

    这仅仅是汤米·道格拉斯毕生卓越功勋中的冰山一角。倘若没有他当年的先驱创举，今日的加拿大将呈现出截然不同的冷酷图景。每一位生活在这片土地上的人，都由衷感恩他为塑造一个充满慈爱、公平与温情的加拿大所做出的不朽历史贡献。

    第四节：联邦与省/地区政府的法定分工与原住民医疗行动呼吁 (Pages 23 - 25)

    联邦政府的五大核心法定职责 (The Federal Role in Health Care)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文The Federal Role in Health Care

    The federal government is responsible for the following: • Administering the Canada Health Act and providing provincial funding • Providing direct delivery of health care services to specific groups, such as First Nations people living on reserves; Inuit peoples; serving members of the Canadian Forces and the Royal Canadian Mounted Police (RCMP); eligible veterans; and inmates of federal penitentiaries • Developing and carrying out government policy and programs that promote health and prevent disease; for example, the federal government approves drugs, assesses health risks posed by environmental hazards, and provides funds to support public health programs such as prenatal health education • Transferring tax money to the provinces and territories to share the cost of medically necessary health care services • Prohibiting service providers (such as physicians) from billing clients extra charges and user fees and ensuring that all the provinces and territories provide the same quality and type of care.

    CN 译文联邦政府在医疗卫生中的五大法定职责

    根据加拿大宪法与《加拿大健康法案》，联邦政府直接履行以下五项国家层面的重大核心权责： 1. 监管法案执行与划拨财政资金：全权行政主管《加拿大健康法案》的法定遵循情况，依法向各省与地区划拨巨额医疗专项现金转移支付。 2. 向特定法定群体直接提供医疗照护：直接承担特定特殊人群的直接医疗卫生服务交付，包括：居住在保留地内的第一民族（First Nations）原住民；因纽特（Inuit）族群；加拿大武装部队现役官兵；加拿大皇家骑警（RCMP）现役警员；符合法定优抚条件的退役退伍军人；以及联邦监狱在押服刑犯人。 3. 制定国家卫生政策与疾病预防国家战略：负责国家层面的处方药品与疫苗上市审批、评估环境有害因素造成的健康风险，并出资资助国家级公共卫生计划（如产前优生优育科普教育）。 4. 税收转移支付共担医疗成本：通过联邦医疗转移支付（CHT），将中央税收按人口比例返还并拨付给各省及地区，共担必需医疗开销。 5. 铁腕杜绝额外自费乱收费并维护全国统一品质：严格立法禁止任何医疗服务提供者（如执业医师或诊所）向受助案主巧立名目收取额外费用或自费挂号费，确保各省各地区维持全国一致的受保标准与照护品质。

    BOX 2.3 加拿大卫生部在原住民医疗中的历史角色演变 (Health Canada's Role in Indigenous Health Care)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文BOX 2.3: Health Canada's Role in Indigenous Health Care

    - 1945: Health services for this population were transferred from the Department of Indian Affairs to Health Canada. - 1962: Through the new Medical Services Branch, Health Canada started to provide direct health services to First Nations people on reserves and to the Inuit in the North. - 1974: A governmental policy acknowledged that although no treaty required the government to provide health care to Indigenous people, Health Canada would ensure the availability of services. - 1979: A new Indian Health Policy stated that uninsured benefits would rely on "professional medical and dental judgement" and recognized "the need for community development, a strong relationship between First Nations people, the federal government, and the Canadian health system." - Mid-1980s: Work began so that First Nations, Métis, and Inuit communities would have more control of their own health services. - 1998: The Canadian government published Gathering Strength: Canada's Aboriginal Action Plan, which emphasized Health Canada's commitment to diabetes and tuberculosis initiatives, the development of the Aboriginal Healing Foundation, and a healing strategy related to Indian residential schools, in partnership with the Department of Indian Affairs and Northern Development. - 2000: The Medical Services Branch was renamed the First Nations and Inuit Health Branch (FNIHB).

    CN 译文BOX 2.3 历史文献专栏：加拿大卫生部在原住民健康医疗中的演进编年史

    - 1945 年：针对原住民群体的医疗卫生事务正式由原印第安事务部移交至加拿大卫生部统筹主管。 - 1962 年：通过新成立的医疗服务分局（Medical Services Branch），卫生部开始直接为保留地内的第一民族原住民及北部极地因纽特人提供现场面对面的直接医疗卫生服务。 - 1974 年：联邦政府出台官方政策正式确认：即便早期历史条约未曾明确强制规定政府必须提供医疗保障，加拿大卫生部亦将全力确保原住民享有充分的医疗可及性。 - 1979 年：颁布全新《印第安人健康政策》，明确受保范围外的补助福利将依托“专业的医学与牙科临床判断”，并深刻认识到“推进社区发展、在第一民族、联邦政府与加拿大医疗卫生体系之间建立牢固纽带的紧迫必要性”。 - 1980 年代中叶：全面启动赋权进程，使第一民族、梅蒂斯人（Métis）与因纽特社区逐步拥有自主统筹与管理本族群卫生医疗事务的自决控制权。 - 1998 年：加拿大政府正式发布《凝聚力量：加拿大原住民行动计划》（Gathering Strength），重点落实卫生部对糖尿病与结核病攻坚防范的战略承诺，成立原住民疗愈基金会，并联合相关部门开展针对“原住民寄宿学校历史创伤”的专项身心疗愈救助。 - 2000 年：正式重组并将医疗服务分局更名为“第一民族与因纽特人健康分局”（FNIHB）。

    BOX 2.4 加拿大真相与和解委员会关于健康的七大呼吁 (TRC Seven Health Calls to Action)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文BOX 2.4: Truth and Reconciliation Commission of Canada's Seven Health Calls to Action

    The Truth and Reconciliation Commission of Canada (2015) identified seven Health Calls to Action (Calls 18 to 24) necessary to redress the legacy of residential schools: 18. We call upon the federal, provincial, territorial, and Aboriginal governments to acknowledge that the current state of Aboriginal health in Canada is a direct result of previous Canadian government policies, including residential schools, and to recognize and implement the health-care rights of Aboriginal people as identified in international law, constitutional law, and under the Treaties. 19. We call upon the federal government, in consultation with Aboriginal peoples, to establish measurable goals to identify and close the gaps in health outcomes between Aboriginal and non-Aboriginal communities, and to publish annual progress reports and assess long-term trends... 20. In order to address the jurisdictional disputes concerning Aboriginal people who do not reside on reserves, we call upon the federal government to recognize, respect, and address the distinct health needs of the Métis, Inuit, and off-reserve Aboriginal peoples. 21. We call upon the federal government to provide sustainable funding for existing and new Aboriginal healing centres to address the physical, mental, emotional, and spiritual harms caused by residential schools, and to ensure that the funding of healing centres in Nunavut and the Northwest Territories is a priority. 22. We call upon those who can effect change within the Canadian health-care system to recognize the value of Aboriginal healing practices and use them in the treatment of Aboriginal patients in collaboration with Aboriginal healers and Elders where requested by Aboriginal patients. 23. We call upon all levels of government to: (i) Increase the number of Aboriginal professionals working in the health-care field; (ii) Ensure the retention of Aboriginal health-care providers in Aboriginal communities; (iii) Provide cultural competency training for all healthcare professionals. 24. We call upon medical and nursing schools in Canada to require all students to take a course dealing with Aboriginal health issues, including the history and legacy of residential schools, the United Nations Declaration on the Rights of Indigenous Peoples, Treaties and Aboriginal rights, and Indigenous teachings and practices.

    CN 译文BOX 2.4 重大人道文献：加拿大真相与和解委员会（TRC）关于医疗卫生的七项历史性行动呼吁

    加拿大真相与和解委员会（TRC，2015年）明确提出了消除原住民寄宿学校深重创伤所必须彻底践行的七大卫生健康行动呼吁（第 18 条至第 24 条）： - 第 18 条：呼吁联邦、省、地区及原住民政府共同承认：加拿大原住民当前的健康劣势与困境是过去政府错误政策（包括强迫寄宿学校）的直接恶果；并切实承认并全面落实国际法、宪法及历史条约所确立的原住民医疗健康权利。 - 第 19 条：呼吁联邦政府与原住民深入协商，设立明确可衡量的考核指标，彻底消除原住民与非原住民社区之间的健康差距（涵盖婴儿死亡率、产妇健康、自杀干预、心理健康、成瘾康复及慢性病防治等），并每年向社会公开进展报告。 - 第 20 条：呼吁联邦政府妥善解决针对非保留地原住民的管辖权法律争议，切实尊重并满足梅蒂斯人（Métis）、因纽特人及离乡非保留地原住民的独特健康需求。 - 第 21 条：呼吁联邦政府为既有及新建的原住民疗愈中心提供持续长效的财政资助，重点修复寄宿学校所遗留的身体、心理、情绪与灵性四重创伤，并优先保障努纳武特（Nunavut）与西北地区疗愈中心的充足资金。 - 第 22 条：呼吁加拿大医疗系统决策者充分承认原住民传统医学疗愈体系的崇高价值；在原住民病患主动提出要求时，医护人员必须尊重其意愿，主动邀请原住民传统草药师与长者（Elders）协同开展联合诊治。 - 第 23 条：呼吁各级政府：(i) 大力扩招并培养原住民医护专业人才；(ii) 确保原住民医护工作者长期扎根并服务于原住民本地社区；(iii) 为全体在职医护人员强制开展文化胜任力（Cultural Competency）与反种族歧视专业培训。 - 第 24 条：呼吁全加所有医学院与护士院校，强制要求全体在校生必修原住民健康专题课程，系统研习寄宿学校历史创伤、联合国《原住民权利宣言》以及原住民传统医学智慧。

    第五节：医疗服务交付的三大层级与向居家照护的战略重心转移 (Pages 25 - 28)

    初级、二级、三级与四级医疗服务全貌 (Primary, Secondary, Tertiary & Quaternary Care)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文Primary, Secondary, and Tertiary Health Care Delivery

    In the past, most health care funding went to hospitals and equipment, and the focus of health care was to make people better after they became ill. The Canadian government has recognized that secondary or tertiary health care delivery, while still important, can be shortened or prevented altogether with adequate preventive measures taken in the primary health care delivery stage. Every province and territory provides primary, secondary, and tertiary health care (FIGURE 2.3).

    • Primary health care delivery is the first point of contact that most people have with the health care system (e.g., through a doctor, a nurse, or another health care provider, or perhaps through phone-based or computer-based services). Primary health care delivery usually takes place in the community, but it can also take place within a hospital. Primary health care delivery offers a wide range of services, focusing mostly on health promotion, early diagnosis of disease or disability, and disease prevention. Coordinated primary health care teams include family doctors, nurses, nurse practitioners, and other health care providers.

    • Secondary health care delivery includes assessment, diagnosis, treatment, and preventive services that are associated with more complex medical issues. This type of care is generally provided by specialist physicians and other specialized health care providers. An example of secondary health care delivery would be a referral from a family doctor or nurse practitioner to a medical specialist for a specific medical issue that may require diagnosis and treatment. It can take place in the community (such as the specialist doctor's office) or in a hospital.

    • Tertiary health care delivery is a specialized, highly technical level of health care that takes place in large research and teaching hospitals. Tertiary health care delivery usually takes place within specialized care units such as a critical care unit and requires advanced diagnostic and treatment support services as well as highly specialized personnel. It is the most expensive level of health care delivery.

    • A new specialized section of tertiary health care delivery is called quaternary care, which includes experimental medicines or surgical procedures. Because it is so highly specialized, not every health care centre will offer quaternary care.

    CN 译文初级、二级、三级与四级医疗服务交付全景

    在历史上，加拿大绝大部分医疗财政资金曾全盘涌向大型医院和重型设备，传统医疗理念的重心局限于病人在患病后前往医院求医。然而，现代加拿大政府深刻意识到：二级专科医疗与三级重症医疗固然至关重要，但若能在“初级卫生保健”阶段落实充分的前置预防，绝大多数急难重症完全可以被预防、推迟甚至彻底避免！在加拿大每个省份与地区，均完整提供以下梯次医疗服务（参见图 2.3）：

    • 初级卫生保健（Primary Care）：绝大多数公民与医疗体系发生的第一道接触点（例如家庭医生门诊、诊所执业护士、社区卫生服务中心或致电 Telehealth 远程健康热线）。初级照护主要立足于社区，亦可在社区医院门诊开展，其核心聚焦于健康科普宣教、疾病或残障的极早期筛查确诊、以及前置预防干预。紧密协作的初级团队由家庭医生、注册护士、执业高级护士（NP）及专职健康人员组成。

    • 二级专业专科医疗（Secondary Care）：涵盖针对更为复杂的疑难医学健康问题所展开的专科评估、鉴别诊断与专项治疗。此类照护由专科医生（Specialists，如心脏科专科医师、骨科专科医师）与各专科医护团队担纲。最典型的途径是：家庭医生在初诊发现病情复杂后，依法向病患出具转诊介绍信（Referral），转介至专科诊所或综合医院接受深层次专科诊治。

    • 三级高阶专精重症医疗（Tertiary Care）：在大型医科大学附属研究型教学医院中开展的高精尖、高技术密集型专精医疗服务。三级照护通常依托于医院内的特殊专科监护单元（如心脏重症 ICU、神经外科手术室），需要尖端精密检查设备和国家级医学专家团队保驾护航。这是整个医疗体系中运营成本最为高昂、技术难度最高的医疗层级。

    • 四级超特前沿医疗（Quaternary Care）：作为三级专精医疗的最新延伸层级，四级医疗专指在极少数顶尖学术科研中心开展的前沿实验性药物临床试验、极罕见病尖端基因疗法或开创性超高难度外科手术。由于其极其专精前沿，绝大多数普通医院并不设立四级医疗单元。

    医院向社区居家照护的战略重心大转移 (The Historical Shift to Home Care)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文Home Care

    Over the years, the Canadian health care system has seen a shift in focus from hospital care to home care. Traditionally, people entered the health care system through hospitals. However, over the past two decades, the role and structure of hospitals have changed dramatically. Operating a hospital requires a tremendous amount of money. Over a third of all health care spending goes into running hospitals. Therefore, in order to cut costs, most provincial and territorial governments have reduced the number of hospitals: hundreds of hospitals have closed, merged, or been converted into other types of health care facilities.

    Partly to save money and partly as a result of technological advances, and also to reduce infection rates, clients are now sent home sooner after hospital procedures than they used to be. Each year, fewer clients stay in hospital overnight, and if they do stay overnight, they stay for shorter time periods than they would have in the past. In order to support patients who leave hospitals early, governments have gradually increased spending on home care. Home care is health care and support services provided to people in their places of residence, including private homes, licensed live-in care facilities, and assisted-living facilities (see Chapter 3). Home care is the most common of the community-based services, and support workers are needed and are actively recruited to work in all home care areas.

    Home care was first created to provide care for people who needed at-home assistance after hospital discharge. Today, home care provides community care and support to a wide range of people. Clients include older persons; families with children; people who are living with mental, physical, or developmental disabilities; people with short-term and long-term medical conditions; and people in the recovery, rehabilitative, or final stages of a life-ending disease. Home care services provide assistance to families who need help with a new baby. They enable people with disabilities to get up in the morning and get ready for school or work. They help people adjust to a disability or recover from an illness (FIGURE 2.4). They enable people who are dying to remain at home rather than be admitted to a hospital.

    One major focus of home care is to enable people to remain in their homes, so they can be as healthy as possible and remain independent for as long as possible. For some people, home care replaces hospital or other facility care. For others, home care allows them to maintain their health and independence, thus delaying or preventing admission to a facility.

    CN 译文居家照护（Home Care）的崛起与战略重心大转移

    纵观近数十年的发展历程，加拿大医疗卫生系统经历了一场从以传统医院为中心向以社区居家照护为核心的深层次历史性战略大转移。在传统观念中，医院是人们步入医疗体系的唯一主门户。然而在过去二十年间，医院的社会定位与组织架构发生了翻天覆地的剧变。维持一家现代化综合医院的高昂运营需要天文数字般的资金支持——全加超过三分之一的公共医疗开支被消耗在医院运转之中！为了遏制医疗成本的恶性膨胀，绝大多数省份与地区政府断然缩减了急性病床与医院数量：全加上百家医院被依法关停、合并或改建为康复慢病中心。

    缩减开支的财政刚性需求、现代微创医疗技术的突飞猛进、以及降低医院院内交叉感染率（Nosocomial Infection）的临床要求，共同促成了病患在接受手术后被极快安排出院返回居家的崭新常态。现如今，不仅留院过夜的病患人数逐年锐减，且病患的平均住院日（Length of Stay）相比过去大幅缩水。为了切实兜底并支撑那些提前出院返回家庭的脆弱病患，各省政府将原本用于医院的庞大财政预算持续转移并大幅倾斜至“居家照护”领域！居家照护，是指在服务对象个人居住场所（包括其私人独栋居所、持牌机构住宅或辅助生活居所）内所交付的医疗卫生与全方位生活照料。居家照护已成为加拿大最主流的社区服务形态，一线支持工作者（PSW）更成为全加各地最为紧缺、招聘最为炙手可热的刚需专业人才！

    居家照护最初设立的初衷，纯粹是为了向出院返家的病患提供过渡期的伤口擦拭与生活协助。而在当今时代，居家照护的服务对象已全方位扩展至全社会的广阔人群：包括独居或患病的高龄长者；抚育婴幼儿的困难家庭；身患心理精神障碍、肢体残疾或智力发育障碍的人士；饱受各类急慢性顽疾困扰的病患；处于创伤康复期的伤者；以及身患绝症处于生命终末期的临终安宁关怀案主。居家照护人员协助年轻母亲为新生儿沐浴换尿布；协助高位截瘫青年清晨起床离床、穿衣洗漱以按时出门上班上学；搀扶中风长者重新适应生活（参见图 2.4）；并让身患晚期癌症的长者能够安详尊严地在自己熟悉的家庭床榻上走完生命最后一程，免除凄凉死在冰冷病房中的遗憾。

    居家照护的核心执业宗旨，在于赋能人们有尊严地长久留居在自己的家庭之中，尽一切可能维系其最佳身体健康，并在最长的时间尺度内捍卫其独立自主的生活自理能力。对许多案主而言，上门居家照护彻底替代了高昂的住院；对更多长者而言，居家照护维系了其身心功能，从而大幅推迟甚至彻底避免了被无奈送入养老院（长期照护机构）的命运。

    第六节：居家照护服务四大范畴与省/地区职责分工 (Pages 27 - 29)

    居家照护的核心分类体系 (Four Categories of Home Care Services)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文Home Care Services and Funding

    Support workers provide most home care support services. In most provinces and territories, support services are provided by both public and private agencies that can be either for-profit or not-for-profit organizations. Every province and territory has a publicly funded home care program... All provinces and territories offer: • Client assessment—determining if the person is eligible for services • Case coordination and management (see Chapter 10) • Nursing services • Support services for eligible clients

    Home care services are classified into the following categories: 1. Personal care services: These nonmedical services offered through home care, often by support workers, include assisting with activities of daily living (ADLs—e.g., bathing, feeding, mobility, and dressing) and providing comfort care to clients who are dying. 2. Home support services: These services, often provided by support workers to clients who live at home, include assisting with home management, assisting with ADLs, and assisting with taking medications. 3. Professional services: Therapies and treatments provided by the relevant health care providers, which include nursing care, physiotherapy, occupational therapy, speech therapy, nutrition counselling, social work, respiratory therapy, paramedic and emergency medical technician (EMT) services, nail care, and in-home blood testing. 4. Support for instrumental activities of daily living (IADLs): These services, often provided by support workers, include shopping with a client, assisting a client with banking, and teaching a client how to follow a recipe.

    Volunteer services such as Meals on Wheels (FIGURE 2.5) and friendly visiting can be provided by anyone who meets the volunteer criteria.

    CN 译文居家照护四大核心服务范畴与公共资助体系

    在加拿大一线医疗实践中，支持工作者承担了绝大多数的居家支持操作。在各省与地区，上门服务是由公立直管机构以及受政府资助委派的私立商业性（For-profit）或非营利性（Not-for-profit）照护机构协同执行。所有省份均无一例外地设立了由公共税收资助的官方居家照护体系，统一向公众提供以下核心服务： • 案主专业评估：由个案经理全面上门评估，判定案主是否符合政府免费居家照护的准入资格； • 个案统筹与临床管理：制定个性化长期照护方案（参见第 10 章）； • 专业上门护理：派遣注册护士处理复杂换药与注射； • 合格案主的一线生活支持服务：派遣支持工作者上门提供日常生活照料。

    具体而言，加拿大官方将居家照护细分为以下四大核心职能范畴： 1. 个人身体照护服务（Personal Care Services）：主要由支持工作者执行的非技术性个人生理照料，涵盖基础日常生活活动（ADLs——如盆浴淋浴擦浴、喂饭协助、床上轴线翻身与轮椅转移、协助穿脱衣物及个人梳理修饰），以及为临终关怀案主提供舒适抚慰护理。 2. 家庭支持维护服务（Home Support Services）：支持工作者在案主家中开展的家居环境支持，包括协助家庭日常事务管理、整理床铺、基础卫生打扫、协助日常生活活动以及协助督导案主按时自服药物。 3. 专业医护执业服务（Professional Services）：由持牌注册医护人员登门提供的技术性诊疗与康复服务，包括：专业伤口护理与导尿管更换、物理治疗（PT）、作业康复治疗（OT）、言语语言治疗（ST）、临床营养配餐咨询、注册社会工作者心理疏导、呼吸治疗、急救技术员（EMT）转运、专业医疗修甲、以及上门静脉抽血检验等。 4. 工具性日常生活支持服务（Support for IADLs）：支持工作者协助维持案主在社区独立生活所需的高阶技能，包括：陪伴案主前往超市采购食品杂货、协助案主前往银行网点处理存取款理财账单、以及耐心指导案主依照健康食谱学习膳食烹饪。

    此外，社会志愿服务（如著名的“流动爱心送餐”Meals on Wheels，参见图 2.5）以及长者温情结对走访，则由符合资历的民间社会义工通力承担。

    BOX 2.5 居家照护的行政管理与交付模式 (How Home Care Is Governed and Delivered)

    ▶ 听此段🔊 正在朗读
    EN 原文BOX 2.5: How Home Care Is Governed and Delivered

    How home care is governed and delivered differs across the provinces and territories. In all jurisdictions, the ministries or departments of health and social or community services are responsible for home care services. These departments monitor the services and decide on budgets, policies, and standards of care. Service delivery involves the following: • Assessing clients' needs • Determining clients' eligibility for government-sponsored professional and support services • Coordinating and monitoring home care services provided by private or not-for-profit agencies • Providing information and referrals to other long-term care services (e.g., volunteer-based community services such as Meals on Wheels), some of which charge user fees to the client • Providing placement services to assisted-living facilities and extended care facilities (known in some provinces as long-term care facilities)

    CN 译文BOX 2.5 制度专栏：加拿大各省居家照护的行政主管与服务交付机制

    各省与地区在居家照护的行政管理体制与具体落地模式上呈现出鲜明的区域特色。但在所有辖区内，省卫生厅（Ministry of Health）以及社会与社区服务厅均牢牢掌握居家照护的最终行政领导权，负责宏观监督全行业执业水准、严格审批财政预算、制定执业法规并设立全省统一的临床照护标准。各省具体的一线服务交付流程包括以下五大关键环节： • 科学评估受助案主的多维照护需求； • 依法裁定案主是否有资格免费申领政府全额资助的专业护理与生活支持工时； • 严格统筹、调度并持续质控那些由政府购买服务、外包给商业或非营利性机构的一线照护服务； • 为案主提供权威资讯指引，转介至其他长期照护补充服务（如 Meals on Wheels 爱心送餐等民间志愿服务，部分项目仅向案主象征性收取极低原料成本费）； • 全权主导案主入住辅助生活居所（Assisted-Living）及长期照护院舍（Long-Term Care / 养老院）的官方排期评估与轮候安置（Placement Services）。
